\section{1. Load \& Prepare Data}\label{load-prepare-data}

Two changes versus the original \texttt{load\_cs()}:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \textbf{Contiguous integer period index.} The original coded
  \texttt{period} as \texttt{YYYY*100\ +\ MM} (e.g.~202012, 202101).
  \texttt{aggte(type\ =\ "dynamic")} computes event time as
  \texttt{e\ =\ t\ -\ g} from the \emph{raw}
  \texttt{tname}/\texttt{gname} values, so across a year boundary that
  difference is 89, not 1. The \texttt{type\ =\ "simple"} aggregation
  that feeds the headline table is unaffected (it only needs the
  post/pre ordering), but the dynamic event-study plots and the
  \texttt{min\_e}/\texttt{max\_e} windows are distorted. Coding
  \texttt{period} as a dense rank (1, 2, 3, \ldots) makes both
  aggregations correct regardless of \texttt{did} version.
\item
  \textbf{Quarterly aggregation.} When
  \texttt{TIME\_UNIT\ ==\ "quarter"}, raw monthly counts are summed
  within firm-quarter \emph{before} the \texttt{log1p}, and \texttt{g}
  is floored to the quarter that contains the approval date. Aggregating
  the counts and then logging (rather than averaging monthly logs) is
  the correct order for an additive count outcome.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Floor a Date to the start of its time unit}
\NormalTok{floor\_unit }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(d, }\AttributeTok{unit =}\NormalTok{ TIME\_UNIT) \{}
  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (unit }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"month"}\NormalTok{) \{}
    \FunctionTok{as.Date}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{format}\NormalTok{(d, }\StringTok{"\%Y{-}\%m{-}01"}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{  \} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{  }\CommentTok{\# quarter}
\NormalTok{    q  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{as.integer}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{format}\NormalTok{(d, }\StringTok{"\%m"}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \DecValTok{1}\DataTypeTok{L}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%/\%} \DecValTok{3}\DataTypeTok{L}          \CommentTok{\# 0..3}
\NormalTok{    mm }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sprintf}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\%02d"}\NormalTok{, q }\SpecialCharTok{*} \DecValTok{3}\DataTypeTok{L} \SpecialCharTok{+} \DecValTok{1}\DataTypeTok{L}\NormalTok{)                       }\CommentTok{\# 01,04,07,10}
    \FunctionTok{as.Date}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{format}\NormalTok{(d, }\StringTok{"\%Y"}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"{-}"}\NormalTok{, mm, }\StringTok{"{-}01"}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{  \}}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# All n\_collabs* columns are outcome counts; everything else is meta/covariate}
\NormalTok{count\_cols }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(df) }\FunctionTok{grep}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\^{}n\_collabs"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(df), }\AttributeTok{value =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{load\_cs }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(path, }\AttributeTok{unit =}\NormalTok{ TIME\_UNIT) \{}
\NormalTok{  df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read.csv}\NormalTok{(path, }\AttributeTok{stringsAsFactors =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{month }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.Date}\NormalTok{(df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{month)}

\NormalTok{  ccols }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{count\_cols}\NormalTok{(df)}

  \CommentTok{\# Covariates that are constant within firm (carried through aggregation)}
\NormalTok{  firm\_cov }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"g"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"is\_treated"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"is\_control"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"matched\_to"}\NormalTok{,}
                \StringTok{"nace1\_psm"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"nace1\_proxy"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"firm\_type"}\NormalTok{,}
                \StringTok{"log\_staffCount"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"age"}\NormalTok{,}
                \StringTok{"log\_collab\_count\_2020"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"degree\_centrality\_2020"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  firm\_cov }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{intersect}\NormalTok{(firm\_cov, }\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(df))}

  \CommentTok{\# ── Collapse to the chosen time unit ───────────────────────────────────────}
\NormalTok{  df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{tunit }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{floor\_unit}\NormalTok{(df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{month, unit)}

  \CommentTok{\# Level outcomes (per{-}month means, e.g. consortium size) are AVERAGED within}
  \CommentTok{\# firm{-}quarter, NOT summed like the counts. (Absent in old panels {-}\textgreater{} no{-}op.)}
\NormalTok{  level\_cols }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{intersect}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"mean\_collab\_size"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"mean\_collab\_size\_active"}\NormalTok{), }\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(df))}

  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (unit }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"quarter"}\NormalTok{) \{}
\NormalTok{    counts }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(Linkedin\_name, tunit) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{across}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{all\_of}\NormalTok{(ccols), }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(.x, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)),}
                \AttributeTok{.groups =} \StringTok{"drop"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    meta }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(Linkedin\_name) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{across}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{all\_of}\NormalTok{(firm\_cov), }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ dplyr}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{first}\NormalTok{(.x)),}
                \AttributeTok{.groups =} \StringTok{"drop"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    levels\_q }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(level\_cols)) df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(Linkedin\_name, tunit) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{across}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{all\_of}\NormalTok{(level\_cols), }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(.x, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)),  }\CommentTok{\# AVERAGE, not sum}
                \AttributeTok{.groups =} \StringTok{"drop"}\NormalTok{) }\ControlFlowTok{else} \ConstantTok{NULL}
\NormalTok{    df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ counts }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(meta, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{"Linkedin\_name"}\NormalTok{)}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.null}\NormalTok{(levels\_q)) df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(levels\_q, }\AttributeTok{by =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Linkedin\_name"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"tunit"}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{  \} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}
    \CommentTok{\# monthly: tunit == month, nothing to sum, just keep one row per firm{-}month}
    \CommentTok{\# (.keep\_all carries mean\_collab\_size through unchanged)}
\NormalTok{    df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{distinct}\NormalTok{(Linkedin\_name, tunit, }\AttributeTok{.keep\_all =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  \}}

  \CommentTok{\# ── Contiguous integer period index (1..T) ─────────────────────────────────}
\NormalTok{  u\_periods   }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sort}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{tunit))}
\NormalTok{  period\_idx  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{setNames}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{seq\_along}\NormalTok{(u\_periods), }\FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(u\_periods))}
\NormalTok{  df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{period   }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.integer}\NormalTok{(period\_idx[}\FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{tunit)])}

  \CommentTok{\# ── g\_period on the same contiguous scale (0 = never{-}treated) ───────────────}
\NormalTok{  df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{g }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{g)}
\NormalTok{  df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{g[df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{g }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{""} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{g }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"NA"}\NormalTok{] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ConstantTok{NA}
\NormalTok{  g\_unit }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{floor\_unit}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{as.Date}\NormalTok{(df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{g), unit)}
\NormalTok{  df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{g\_period }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ period\_idx[}\FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(g\_unit)]}
\NormalTok{  df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{g\_period[}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{g\_period)] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{0}\DataTypeTok{L}
\NormalTok{  df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{g\_period }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{g\_period)}

\NormalTok{  df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{firm\_id }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.integer}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Linkedin\_name))}

  \CommentTok{\# ── log1p every count column generically {-}\textgreater{} log\_\textless{}name\textgreater{} ─────────────────────}
  \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (cc }\ControlFlowTok{in}\NormalTok{ ccols) \{}
\NormalTok{    df[[}\FunctionTok{sub}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\^{}n\_collabs"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"log\_collabs"}\NormalTok{, cc)]] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{log1p}\NormalTok{(df[[cc]])}
\NormalTok{  \}}

  \CommentTok{\# ── extensive{-}margin (binary) version of every count column ────────────────}
  \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (cc }\ControlFlowTok{in}\NormalTok{ ccols) \{}
\NormalTok{    df[[}\FunctionTok{sub}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\^{}n\_collabs"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"any\_collabs"}\NormalTok{, cc)]] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.integer}\NormalTok{(df[[cc]] }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  \}}

\NormalTok{  df}
\NormalTok{\}}

\NormalTok{battery\_s1  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{load\_cs}\NormalTok{(PATH\_BATTERY\_S1)}
\NormalTok{hydrogen\_s1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{load\_cs}\NormalTok{(PATH\_HYDROGEN\_S1)}
\NormalTok{battery\_s2  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{load\_cs}\NormalTok{(PATH\_BATTERY\_S2)}
\NormalTok{hydrogen\_s2 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{load\_cs}\NormalTok{(PATH\_HYDROGEN\_S2)}

\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"TIME\_UNIT ="}\NormalTok{, TIME\_UNIT, }\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# ============================================================}
\CommentTok{\# Outcome map: which column each technology uses.}
\CommentTok{\# ============================================================}
\CommentTok{\#}
\CommentTok{\#   label                     battery column                 hydrogen column}
\CommentTok{\#   {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}  {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}  {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\#   technology{-}tagged (intens.)  log\_collabs\_battery         log\_collabs\_hydrogen}
\CommentTok{\#   cross{-}border EU           log\_collabs\_eu\_crossborder\_battery  ...\_hydrogen}
\CommentTok{\#   cross{-}sector              log\_collabs\_crosssector\_battery     ...\_hydrogen}
\CommentTok{\#   cross{-}sector (EU only)    log\_collabs\_crosssector\_eu\_battery  ...\_hydrogen   [optional]}
\CommentTok{\#   non{-}EU (placebo)          log\_collabs\_non\_eu\_battery          ...\_hydrogen}
\CommentTok{\#   domestic (placebo)        log\_collabs\_domestic\_battery        ...\_hydrogen}
\CommentTok{\#   within{-}sector (placebo)   log\_collabs\_withinsector\_battery    ...\_hydrogen}
\CommentTok{\#   cross{-}sector (NACE{-}1)     log\_collabs\_crosssector\_nace1\_battery  ...\_hydrogen  [robustness]}
\CommentTok{\#}
\NormalTok{outcome\_map }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{tech\_focused =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{battery =} \StringTok{"log\_collabs\_battery"}\NormalTok{,}
                   \AttributeTok{hydrogen =} \StringTok{"log\_collabs\_hydrogen"}\NormalTok{),}
  \AttributeTok{cross\_border =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{battery =} \StringTok{"log\_collabs\_eu\_crossborder\_battery"}\NormalTok{,}
                   \AttributeTok{hydrogen =} \StringTok{"log\_collabs\_eu\_crossborder\_hydrogen"}\NormalTok{),}
  \AttributeTok{cross\_sector =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{battery =} \StringTok{"log\_collabs\_crosssector\_battery"}\NormalTok{,}
                   \AttributeTok{hydrogen =} \StringTok{"log\_collabs\_crosssector\_hydrogen"}\NormalTok{),}

             \AttributeTok{hydrogen =} \StringTok{"log\_collabs\_non\_eu\_hydrogen"}\NormalTok{),}

\CommentTok{\# Pre{-}trend test. Primary is did\textquotesingle{}s universal Wald pre{-}test (cs\_out$Wpval). For}
\CommentTok{\# staggered designs that bootstrap covariance is singular and att\_gt returns no}
\CommentTok{\# Wald (hydrogen, confirmed {-}{-} it stays NULL under never{-} AND not{-}yet{-}treated).}
\CommentTok{\# In that case fall back to a joint test that the pre{-}period (e \textless{} 0) dynamic}
\CommentTok{\# event{-}study coefficients are zero: a chi{-}square of their t{-}statistics from}
\CommentTok{\# aggte(type="dynamic") att.egt/se.egt (analytic SEs, df = \# pre{-}periods). The}
\CommentTok{\# \textasciigrave{}method\textasciigrave{} field records which test was used.}
\NormalTok{es\_pretrend }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(cs\_out) \{}
  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{is.null}\NormalTok{(cs\_out)) }\FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{stat =} \ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{p =} \ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{method =} \ConstantTok{NA\_character\_}\NormalTok{))}
  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.null}\NormalTok{(cs\_out}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Wpval))}
    \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{stat =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(cs\_out}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{W, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{p =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(cs\_out}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Wpval, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{method =} \StringTok{"Wald"}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{  dyn }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{tryCatch}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{aggte}\NormalTok{(cs\_out, }\AttributeTok{type =} \StringTok{"dynamic"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{min\_e =} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{12}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{max\_e =} \DecValTok{24}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{bstrap =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{cband =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{error =} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(e) }\ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{)}
  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{is.null}\NormalTok{(dyn)) }\FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{stat =} \ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{p =} \ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{method =} \ConstantTok{NA\_character\_}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{  pre }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ dyn}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{egt }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{0}
\NormalTok{  t2  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (dyn}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{att.egt[pre] }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ dyn}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{se.egt[pre])}\SpecialCharTok{\^{}}\DecValTok{2}
\NormalTok{  ok  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{is.finite}\NormalTok{(t2); k }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(ok)}
  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (k }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{stat =} \ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{p =} \ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{method =} \ConstantTok{NA\_character\_}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{  stat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(t2[ok])}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{stat =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(stat, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{p =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{{-}} \FunctionTok{pchisq}\NormalTok{(stat, }\AttributeTok{df =}\NormalTok{ k), }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{method =} \StringTok{"ES{-}joint"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{\}}

                         \AttributeTok{cs\_out =} \ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{df =} \ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{yname =} \ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{) \{}

    \AttributeTok{stringsAsFactors =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{\}}

  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{is.null}\NormalTok{(agg\_dyn)) }\FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{)}
  \FunctionTok{ggdid}\NormalTok{(agg\_dyn, }\AttributeTok{title =}\NormalTok{ title) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{geom\_vline}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{xintercept =} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{linetype =} \StringTok{"dashed"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{colour =} \StringTok{"grey40"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}

\CommentTok{\# Primary C\&S control group: NEVER{-}TREATED for BOTH technologies. Some firms are}
\CommentTok{\# treated in multiple IPCEI rounds, so a not{-}yet{-}treated firm may already be}
\CommentTok{\# affected by an earlier treatment and contaminate the control pool; never{-}treated}
\CommentTok{\# controls avoid this and make the two technologies symmetric. (Battery is a}
\CommentTok{\# single cohort, which independently requires never{-}treated.) The not{-}yet{-}treated}
\CommentTok{\# alternative is reported as one robustness row for hydrogen (section 6).}
\NormalTok{cg\_for }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(technology) }\StringTok{"nevertreated"}

\CommentTok{\# Convenience: run battery + hydrogen for one outcome key from outcome\_map.}
\CommentTok{\# Primary spec: never{-}treated controls + firm{-}level clustering, both technologies.}
\NormalTok{run\_pair }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(key, label) \{}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{battery  =} \FunctionTok{run\_cs}\NormalTok{(battery\_s1,  outcome\_map[[key]][}\StringTok{"battery"}\NormalTok{],}
                      \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Battery S2 {-}{-}"}\NormalTok{, label),}
                      \AttributeTok{control\_group =} \FunctionTok{cg\_for}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"battery"}\NormalTok{),}
                      \AttributeTok{clustervars   =} \StringTok{"firm\_id"}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{hydrogen =} \FunctionTok{run\_cs}\NormalTok{(hydrogen\_s1, outcome\_map[[key]][}\StringTok{"hydrogen"}\NormalTok{],}
                      \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Hydrogen S2 {-}{-}"}\NormalTok{, label),}
                      \AttributeTok{control\_group =} \FunctionTok{cg\_for}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"hydrogen"}\NormalTok{),}
                      \AttributeTok{clustervars   =} \StringTok{"firm\_id"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{\}}
\end{Highlighting}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# ============================================================}
\CommentTok{\# Export tech{-}tagged dynamic ATT(e) in long format for notebook 08.}
\CommentTok{\# The es\_*.pdf files are ggdid\textquotesingle{}s default style; 08 redraws in house}
\CommentTok{\# style from THIS csv. One row per (technology, outcome, event\_time).}
\CommentTok{\# ============================================================}

\CommentTok{\# (technology, outcome{-}label, cs object) for every outcome we want an ES for.}
\CommentTok{\# Labels MUST match the convention notebook 08 filters on. Adjust the label}
\CommentTok{\# strings here if 08 expects a different separator/spelling.}
\NormalTok{es\_spec }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Battery"}\NormalTok{,  }\StringTok{"Technology{-}tagged collaborations"}\NormalTok{, cs\_tech}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{battery),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Hydrogen"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Technology{-}tagged collaborations"}\NormalTok{, cs\_tech}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{hydrogen),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Battery"}\NormalTok{,  }\StringTok{"Cross{-}border EU collaborations"}\NormalTok{,   cs\_cb}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{battery),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Hydrogen"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Cross{-}border EU collaborations"}\NormalTok{,   cs\_cb}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{hydrogen),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Battery"}\NormalTok{,  }\StringTok{"Cross{-}sector collaborations"}\NormalTok{,      cs\_xs}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{battery),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Hydrogen"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Cross{-}sector collaborations"}\NormalTok{,      cs\_xs}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{hydrogen),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Battery"}\NormalTok{,  }\StringTok{"Non{-}EU collaborations"}\NormalTok{,            cs\_neu}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{battery),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Hydrogen"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Non{-}EU collaborations"}\NormalTok{,            cs\_neu}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{hydrogen),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Battery"}\NormalTok{,  }\StringTok{"Domestic collaborations"}\NormalTok{,          cs\_dom}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{battery),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Hydrogen"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Domestic collaborations"}\NormalTok{,          cs\_dom}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{hydrogen),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Battery"}\NormalTok{,  }\StringTok{"Within{-}sector collaborations"}\NormalTok{,     cs\_ws}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{battery),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Hydrogen"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Within{-}sector collaborations"}\NormalTok{,     cs\_ws}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{hydrogen),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Battery"}\NormalTok{,  }\StringTok{"Non{-}funded collaborations"}\NormalTok{,        cs\_nf}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{battery),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Hydrogen"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Non{-}funded collaborations"}\NormalTok{,        cs\_nf}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{hydrogen),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Battery"}\NormalTok{,  }\StringTok{"Cross{-}sector EU{-}only collaborations"}\NormalTok{, cs\_xseu}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{battery),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Hydrogen"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Cross{-}sector EU{-}only collaborations"}\NormalTok{, cs\_xseu}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{hydrogen)}
\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Consortium{-}size mechanism path: compute its C\&S objects HERE so the dynamic}
\CommentTok{\# event study is always exported. Section 10 (which writes consortium\_size\_att.csv)}
\CommentTok{\# runs AFTER this chunk, so its cs\_size does not yet exist on knit {-}{-} the old}
\CommentTok{\# guard on exists("cs\_size") silently dropped these rows. Estimator is IDENTICAL}
\CommentTok{\# to Section 10: cg\_for() control group, no clustering, so the ES matches the ATT.}
\CommentTok{\# Event study uses the ACTIVE{-}month version (intensive margin), matching the}
\CommentTok{\# headline mechanism in Section 10; falls back to the zero{-}filled column on old}
\CommentTok{\# panels that predate 05a adding mean\_collab\_size\_active.}
\NormalTok{SIZE\_ES\_COL }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\StringTok{"mean\_collab\_size\_active"} \SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(battery\_s1))}
  \StringTok{"mean\_collab\_size\_active"} \ControlFlowTok{else} \StringTok{"mean\_collab\_size"}

\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (RUN\_CONSORTIUM }\SpecialCharTok{\&\&} \StringTok{"mean\_collab\_size"} \SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(battery\_s1)) \{}
\NormalTok{  cs\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{battery  =} \FunctionTok{run\_cs}\NormalTok{(battery\_s1,  SIZE\_ES\_COL,}
                      \StringTok{"Battery {-}{-} consortium size (mechanism)"}\NormalTok{,}
                      \AttributeTok{control\_group =} \FunctionTok{cg\_for}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"battery"}\NormalTok{)),}
    \AttributeTok{hydrogen =} \FunctionTok{run\_cs}\NormalTok{(hydrogen\_s1, SIZE\_ES\_COL,}
                      \StringTok{"Hydrogen {-}{-} consortium size (mechanism)"}\NormalTok{,}
                      \AttributeTok{control\_group =} \FunctionTok{cg\_for}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"hydrogen"}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{  es\_spec }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(es\_spec, }\FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Battery"}\NormalTok{,  }\StringTok{"Consortium size (mean firms/collab)"}\NormalTok{, cs\_size}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{battery),}
    \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Hydrogen"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Consortium size (mean firms/collab)"}\NormalTok{, cs\_size}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{hydrogen)}
\NormalTok{  ))}
\NormalTok{\}}

\NormalTok{es\_to\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(tech, outcome, cs\_obj) \{}
  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{is.null}\NormalTok{(cs\_obj)) }\FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  d }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{tryCatch}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{agg\_dynamic}\NormalTok{(cs\_obj), }\AttributeTok{error =} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(e) }\ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{)}
  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{is.null}\NormalTok{(d)) }\FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{)}
  \CommentTok{\# Simultaneous band: aggte() is called with cband = TRUE, so crit.val.egt is}
  \CommentTok{\# the uniform critical value over the whole event{-}time path. Falls back to the}
  \CommentTok{\# pointwise normal value only if the bootstrap did not return one.}
\NormalTok{  z }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.null}\NormalTok{(d}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{crit.val.egt) }\SpecialCharTok{\&\&} \FunctionTok{is.finite}\NormalTok{(d}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{crit.val.egt))}
\NormalTok{    d}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{crit.val.egt }\ControlFlowTok{else} \FunctionTok{qnorm}\NormalTok{(}\FloatTok{0.975}\NormalTok{)}
  \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{label      =} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(tech, }\StringTok{"{-}{-}"}\NormalTok{, outcome),   }\CommentTok{\# match 08\textquotesingle{}s label convention}
    \AttributeTok{Technology =}\NormalTok{ tech,}
    \AttributeTok{Outcome    =}\NormalTok{ outcome,}
    \AttributeTok{e          =}\NormalTok{ d}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{egt,}
    \AttributeTok{att        =}\NormalTok{ d}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{att.egt,}
    \AttributeTok{se         =}\NormalTok{ d}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{se.egt,}
    \AttributeTok{crit\_val   =}\NormalTok{ z,}
    \AttributeTok{ci\_lower   =}\NormalTok{ d}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{att.egt }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ z }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ d}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{se.egt,}
    \AttributeTok{ci\_upper   =}\NormalTok{ d}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{att.egt }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ z }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ d}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{se.egt,}
    \AttributeTok{TimeUnit   =}\NormalTok{ TIME\_UNIT,}
    \AttributeTok{stringsAsFactors =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{\}}

\NormalTok{event\_study\_tt }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{do.call}\NormalTok{(rbind, }\FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(es\_spec, }\ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(s)}
  \FunctionTok{es\_to\_df}\NormalTok{(s[[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]], s[[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{]], s[[}\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{]])))}

\FunctionTok{write.csv}\NormalTok{(event\_study\_tt,}
          \FunctionTok{file.path}\NormalTok{(out\_dir, }\StringTok{"event\_study\_techtagged.csv"}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{row.names =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)}
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{Wrote event\_study\_techtagged.csv:"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(event\_study\_tt), }\StringTok{"rows,"}\NormalTok{,}
    \FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(event\_study\_tt}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{label)), }\StringTok{"outcome{-}technology series}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}

\section{5. Extensive-margin robustness (binary
outcome)}\label{extensive-margin-robustness-binary-outcome}

When a tech-tagged count is mostly zeros, a \texttt{log1p} ATT is
dominated by the zero mass and its magnitude is hard to read as an
elasticity (Chen \& Roth on logs-with-zeros). A linear-probability C\&S
on the binary ``any such tie this period'' outcome is an honest
complement: the ATT is then a change in the probability of forming that
type of tie. Same identification, more interpretable when events are
rare.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{any\_map }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(outcome\_map, }\ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(v) }\FunctionTok{sub}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\^{}log\_collabs"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"any\_collabs"}\NormalTok{, v))}

\NormalTok{ext\_keys }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"tech\_focused"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"cross\_border"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"cross\_sector"}\NormalTok{)  }\CommentTok{\# the ones we care about}
\NormalTok{ext\_rows }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{()}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (key }\ControlFlowTok{in}\NormalTok{ ext\_keys) \{}

\section{6. Robustness
(technology-tagged)}\label{robustness-technology-tagged}

The primary spec (section 4) is the tighter-balance matched sample
(\texttt{battery\_s1} = 1:3, \texttt{hydrogen\_s1} = cal20\_r2) with
never-treated controls + firm-level clustering. Robustness reuses
\texttt{run\_cs()} + \texttt{outcome\_map}, the contiguous period index
and the firm-quarter aggregation (inheriting the \texttt{TIME\_UNIT}
toggle) and reports two checks: (a) \textbf{larger control group} -- the
SAME never-treated + firm-clustered estimator re-run on the higher-ratio
power matched sample (\texttt{battery\_s2} = 1:7, \texttt{hydrogen\_s2}
= cal15\_r3); and (b) \textbf{not-yet-treated controls} as one shown
alternative (hydrogen only -- battery is a single cohort with no
not-yet-treated units), so the reader sees the primary never-treated
choice does not drive the results. The table is parallel to section 4.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Outcomes to re{-}run {-}{-} mirrors the section{-}4 spec list (the optional}
\CommentTok{\# cross\_sector\_eu outcome is skipped here).}
\NormalTok{rob\_spec }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"tech\_focused"}\NormalTok{,  }\StringTok{"Technology{-}tagged collaborations"}\NormalTok{),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"cross\_border"}\NormalTok{,  }\StringTok{"Cross{-}border EU collaborations"}\NormalTok{),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"cross\_sector"}\NormalTok{,  }\StringTok{"Cross{-}sector collaborations"}\NormalTok{),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"cross\_sector\_nace1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Cross{-}sector collaborations (NACE{-}1)"}\NormalTok{),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"non\_eu"}\NormalTok{,        }\StringTok{"Non{-}EU collaborations"}\NormalTok{),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"domestic"}\NormalTok{,      }\StringTok{"Domestic collaborations"}\NormalTok{),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"within\_sector"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Within{-}sector collaborations"}\NormalTok{),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"non\_funded"}\NormalTok{,    }\StringTok{"Non{-}funded collaborations"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# One robustness ATT row: pick the tech{-}tagged column from outcome\_map, run C\&S}
\CommentTok{\# with the requested variant, return a section{-}4{-}parallel row (NULL{-}safe).}
\NormalTok{rob\_one }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(df, key, technology, outcome, variant,}
                    \AttributeTok{control\_group =} \ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{clustervars =} \ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{) \{}
  \CommentTok{\# Default to the primary control group (never{-}treated, via cg\_for). The}
  \CommentTok{\# "Not{-}yet{-}treated controls" variant passes control\_group explicitly to override.}
  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{is.null}\NormalTok{(control\_group)) control\_group }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{cg\_for}\NormalTok{(technology)}
\NormalTok{  yn }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ outcome\_map[[key]][}\FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(technology)]}
\NormalTok{  cs }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{run\_cs}\NormalTok{(df, yn,}
               \AttributeTok{label         =} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(technology, }\StringTok{"{-}{-}"}\NormalTok{, outcome, }\StringTok{"{-}{-}"}\NormalTok{, variant),}
               \AttributeTok{control\_group =}\NormalTok{ control\_group,}
               \AttributeTok{clustervars   =}\NormalTok{ clustervars)}
  \FunctionTok{make\_att\_row}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{agg\_simple}\NormalTok{(cs), technology, outcome, variant, cs, df, yn)}
\NormalTok{\}}

\NormalTok{rob\_rows }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{()}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (s }\ControlFlowTok{in}\NormalTok{ rob\_spec) \{}
\NormalTok{  key }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ s[[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]]; oc }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ s[[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{]]}
\NormalTok{  rob\_rows }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(rob\_rows, }\FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
    \CommentTok{\# (a) larger control group (power spec, S2): SAME primary estimator}
    \CommentTok{\#     (never{-}treated + firm clustering) on the higher{-}ratio matched sample}
    \FunctionTok{rob\_one}\NormalTok{(battery\_s2,  key, }\StringTok{"Battery"}\NormalTok{,  oc, }\StringTok{"Larger control group"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{clustervars =} \StringTok{"firm\_id"}\NormalTok{),}
    \FunctionTok{rob\_one}\NormalTok{(hydrogen\_s2, key, }\StringTok{"Hydrogen"}\NormalTok{, oc, }\StringTok{"Larger control group"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{clustervars =} \StringTok{"firm\_id"}\NormalTok{),}
    \CommentTok{\# (b) not{-}yet{-}treated controls (hydrogen only; battery is a single cohort with}
    \CommentTok{\#     no not{-}yet{-}treated units) {-}{-} shows the never{-}treated primary choice does}
    \CommentTok{\#     not drive results}
    \FunctionTok{rob\_one}\NormalTok{(hydrogen\_s1, key, }\StringTok{"Hydrogen"}\NormalTok{, oc, }\StringTok{"Not{-}yet{-}treated controls"}\NormalTok{,}
            \AttributeTok{control\_group =} \StringTok{"notyettreated"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{clustervars =} \StringTok{"firm\_id"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  ))}
\NormalTok{\}}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{7. Rosenbaum Bounds
(technology-tagged)}\label{rosenbaum-bounds-technology-tagged}

Method preserved exactly from the original file: collapse each matched
set to a post-treatment pair difference (treated firm vs the mean of its
matched controls, on \texttt{log1p} of the count) and run a Wilcoxon
signed-rank sensitivity analysis with \texttt{rbounds::psens} /
\texttt{hlsens}. It reads the matched-firms and cs\_ready CSVs directly
and works off the raw \texttt{month} / \texttt{g} dates, so it is a
matched-pair analysis unaffected by the period coding; only the
tech-tagged \textbf{count} columns (\texttt{n\_collabs\_*}, not the
logs) are fed in. Outputs go to the \texttt{TIME\_UNIT} results folder.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{run\_rosenbaum }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(matched\_firms\_path, cs\_panel\_path,}
                           \AttributeTok{outcome\_col =} \StringTok{"n\_collabs\_battery"}\NormalTok{,}
                           \AttributeTok{gamma\_max   =} \FloatTok{3.0}\NormalTok{,}
                           \AttributeTok{gamma\_inc   =} \FloatTok{0.1}\NormalTok{,}
                           \AttributeTok{label       =} \StringTok{""}\NormalTok{) \{}

  \FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{=== Rosenbaum Bounds:"}\NormalTok{, label, }\StringTok{"===}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{  firms }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read.csv}\NormalTok{(matched\_firms\_path, }\AttributeTok{stringsAsFactors =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  panel }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read.csv}\NormalTok{(cs\_panel\_path,      }\AttributeTok{stringsAsFactors =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  panel}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{month }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.Date}\NormalTok{(panel}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{month)}

\NormalTok{  g\_lookup }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ panel }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(is\_treated }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

    \FunctionTok{rename}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{g\_treated =}\NormalTok{ g)}

\NormalTok{  panel\_sym }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ panel }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      g\_lookup }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(matched\_to, g\_treated),}

\NormalTok{    ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

\NormalTok{    ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{y =} \FunctionTok{log1p}\NormalTok{(.data[[outcome\_col]])) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(Linkedin\_name) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \CommentTok{\# DiD contrast, not a post{-}treatment level: each firm\textquotesingle{}s own post{-}minus{-}pre}
    \CommentTok{\# change. Pairing these changes makes the Rosenbaum null correspond to the}
    \CommentTok{\# difference{-}in{-}differences estimand rather than to a level difference.}
    \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{y\_mean =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(y[post], }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(y[}\SpecialCharTok{!}\NormalTok{post], }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
              \AttributeTok{.groups =} \StringTok{"drop"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.finite}\NormalTok{(y\_mean))}

\NormalTok{  firms }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ firms }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(panel\_sym, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{"Linkedin\_name"}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{  treated }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ firms }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(is\_treated }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

\NormalTok{  controls\_agg }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ firms }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(is\_control }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

\NormalTok{  psens\_df    }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.data.frame}\NormalTok{(ps}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{bounds)}
\NormalTok{  gamma\_break }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ psens\_df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Gamma[}\FunctionTok{which}\NormalTok{(psens\_df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Lower Bound}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}} \SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{)[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]]}

\CommentTok{\# Dynamic selection: test exactly the outcomes that are significant in THIS run}

\NormalTok{  \})}

\section{8. Benjamini--Hochberg Correction
(technology-tagged)}\label{benjaminihochberg-correction-technology-tagged}

BH applied \textbf{within each value chain}, across that chain's
outcomes, using the p-values from the section-4 \texttt{att\_table}
(computed under the current \texttt{TIME\_UNIT}).

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{bh\_stars }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(p) }\FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(p), }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{,}
                        \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(p }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \FloatTok{0.01}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"***"}\NormalTok{,}
                        \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(p }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"**"}\NormalTok{,}
                        \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(p }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \FloatTok{0.10}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"*"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{))))}

\NormalTok{make\_bh\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(tech) \{}
\NormalTok{  sub   }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ att\_table[att\_table}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Technology }\SpecialCharTok{==}\NormalTok{ tech, ]}
\NormalTok{  p\_raw }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sub}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p\_value}
\NormalTok{  p\_bh  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{p.adjust}\NormalTok{(p\_raw, }\AttributeTok{method =} \StringTok{"BH"}\NormalTok{)}
  \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{technology =}\NormalTok{ tech,}
    \AttributeTok{outcome    =}\NormalTok{ sub}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Outcome,}
    \AttributeTok{p\_raw      =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(p\_raw, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{p\_bh       =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(p\_bh,  }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{sig\_raw    =} \FunctionTok{bh\_stars}\NormalTok{(p\_raw),}
    \AttributeTok{sig\_bh     =} \FunctionTok{bh\_stars}\NormalTok{(p\_bh),}
    \AttributeTok{stringsAsFactors =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{\}}

\NormalTok{bh\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{rbind}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{make\_bh\_df}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Battery"}\NormalTok{), }\FunctionTok{make\_bh\_df}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Hydrogen"}\NormalTok{))}

\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{=== Benjamini{-}Hochberg (per value chain,"}\NormalTok{, TIME\_UNIT, }\StringTok{") ===}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{""}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}

\section{9. Power Simulation (dynamic --- significant outcomes
only)}\label{power-simulation-dynamic-significant-outcomes-only}

Design-based power simulation ported verbatim from the original file:
synthetic treated firms are drawn from the matched controls reproducing
the real cohort sizes, a constant additive effect is imposed on the log
outcome over the post-period, the C\&S overall ATT is re-estimated per
draw, and the rejection rate at the 5\% level is recorded. The
methodology and the per-effect-size draw count are unchanged. The only
change: it runs \textbf{only on the outcomes flagged as findings} in
section 4 (\texttt{p\_value\ \textless{}\ SIG\_THRESHOLD}), mapped back
to their \texttt{outcome\_map} keys, and the combined plot shows just
those, each with a vertical line at its estimated ATT.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{run\_power\_sim }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(cs\_data, outcome\_var, att\_true,}
                          \AttributeTok{n\_sims =} \DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{label =} \StringTok{""}\NormalTok{) \{}
\NormalTok{  controls\_df   }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cs\_data[cs\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{is\_control }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, ]}
\NormalTok{  control\_names }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(controls\_df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Linkedin\_name)}

\NormalTok{  real\_cohorts }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cs\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(is\_treated }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{distinct}\NormalTok{(Linkedin\_name, g\_period) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{count}\NormalTok{(g\_period) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(g\_period)}

\NormalTok{  results }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{numeric}\NormalTok{(n\_sims)}

  \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (i }\ControlFlowTok{in} \FunctionTok{seq\_len}\NormalTok{(n\_sims)) \{}
\NormalTok{    available    }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ control\_names}
\NormalTok{    treated\_pool }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{character}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    g\_vec        }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{integer}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)}

    \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (k }\ControlFlowTok{in} \FunctionTok{seq\_len}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(real\_cohorts))) \{}
\NormalTok{      n\_k    }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ real\_cohorts}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{n[k]}
\NormalTok{      cohort }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ real\_cohorts}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{g\_period[k]}
      \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(available) }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ n\_k) }\ControlFlowTok{break}
\NormalTok{      sampled      }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(available, n\_k, }\AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{      available    }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{setdiff}\NormalTok{(available, sampled)}
\NormalTok{      treated\_pool }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(treated\_pool, sampled)}
\NormalTok{      g\_vec        }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(g\_vec, }\FunctionTok{rep}\NormalTok{(cohort, n\_k))}
\NormalTok{    \}}

\NormalTok{    g\_lookup }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{Linkedin\_name =}\NormalTok{ treated\_pool,}
                           \AttributeTok{g\_sim =}\NormalTok{ g\_vec,}
                           \AttributeTok{stringsAsFactors =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{    sim\_treated }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ controls\_df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(Linkedin\_name }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ treated\_pool) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(g\_lookup, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{"Linkedin\_name"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{post\_sim     =} \FunctionTok{as.integer}\NormalTok{(period }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}=}\NormalTok{ g\_sim),}
             \AttributeTok{outcome\_sim  =}\NormalTok{ .data[[outcome\_var]] }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ att\_true }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ post\_sim,}
             \AttributeTok{g\_period\_sim =}\NormalTok{ g\_sim)}

\NormalTok{    sim\_controls }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ controls\_df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(Linkedin\_name }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ available) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{outcome\_sim  =}\NormalTok{ .data[[outcome\_var]],}
             \AttributeTok{g\_period\_sim =} \DecValTok{0}\DataTypeTok{L}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{    sim\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{bind\_rows}\NormalTok{(sim\_treated, sim\_controls) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{firm\_id\_sim =} \FunctionTok{as.integer}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(Linkedin\_name)))}

\NormalTok{    result }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{tryCatch}\NormalTok{(\{}
\NormalTok{      cs\_sim }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{att\_gt}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{yname         =} \StringTok{"outcome\_sim"}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{tname         =} \StringTok{"period"}\NormalTok{,}

\section{10. Consortium size (mechanism
check)}\label{consortium-size-mechanism-check}

Did treated firms join LARGER multi-firm collaborations after approval?
This is a potential mechanical driver of the composition results.
\texttt{mean\_collab\_size} is the mean number of firms per
collaboration a firm participates in, per period --- a \textbf{level},
not a count and not a share. It is run on the RAW column: no
\texttt{log1p}, not in \texttt{outcome\_map}. Technology-specific
control group via \texttt{cg\_for()} (battery \texttt{nevertreated},
hydrogen \texttt{notyettreated}); \texttt{anticipation\ =\ 0} is already
set inside \texttt{run\_cs()}. \texttt{TIME\_UNIT}-aware (quarterly
averages the per-month means).

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Two versions, see the note above:}
\CommentTok{\#   mean\_collab\_size\_active {-}{-} NA in firm{-}months without a collaboration, so C\&S}
\CommentTok{\#     drops them and the ATT is INTENSIVE margin. This is the headline mechanism.}
\CommentTok{\#   mean\_collab\_size        {-}{-} zero{-}filled, mixes extensive and intensive margins.}
\CommentTok{\#     Reported alongside so the difference between the two is visible.}

\section{Notes for carrying this into your existing sections
5--10}\label{notes-for-carrying-this-into-your-existing-sections-510}

Your downstream sections need only a \textbf{column-name swap} (and they
inherit the contiguous index + quarterly toggle for free, because they
all reuse the data frames and \texttt{run\_cs()} defined above):

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Robustness S1 / clustering / never-treated:} replace each
  \texttt{yname\ =\ "..."} with the tech-tagged column from
  \texttt{outcome\_map}, e.g.
  \texttt{outcome\_map\$cross\_border{[}"hydrogen"{]}}.
\item
  \textbf{Power simulation:} point \texttt{outcome\_var} at the
  tech-tagged column; the zero share is higher, so expect the minimum
  detectable effect to rise --- this is exactly the quantity to report
  when justifying the quarterly check.
\item
  \textbf{Rosenbaum bounds:} unchanged mechanics; just feed the
  tech-tagged count.
\item
  \textbf{Export for Python:} dynamic exports use the corrected
  event-time index now, so your \texttt{08\_ck\_visualisation-results}
  event studies will be right at year boundaries.
\end{itemize}

\end{document}
